Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/[DecryptText]

[DecryptText]

Animates field labels by scrambling through randomized matrix cipher characters before resolving letter-by-letter.

Package: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes Targets: Field | Property AllowMultiple: False Inherited: True Performance: High (Efficient UI Toolkit Text Transitions)

1. Overview & Value Proposition

What it does: [DecryptText] adds a sci-fi hacker or cyberpunk terminal decoding effect to inspector labels and string fields. Upon mounting or clicking the field (TriggerOnFocus), the text begins in a fully scrambled matrix cipher, displaying randomly cycling symbols from a designated character set (ScrambleChars) rendered in a phosphor green glow (GlitchColor, defaulting to #00FF66). At a rate determined by ResolveSpeed (defaulting to 20 characters per second), glyphs resolve sequentially from left to right into their actual names, restoring the original font styling once fully decoded.

The Problem: In sci-fi, espionage, or hacking-themed projects, standard inspector panels appear flat and disconnected from the game's aesthetic. Highlighting classified game state, security clearance levels, or encrypted parameters with standard static labels fails to evoke high-tech immersion.

The Solution: Decorating encrypted parameters or string collections with [DecryptText]:

  • Sci-Fi Matrix Decoding: Progressively unscrambles text strings with rapid character churning.
  • Collection Element Cascading: Implements ICollectionElementAttribute so array and list string elements unscramble independently when viewed or inspected.
  • Customizable Cipher Alphabet: Swap out the default punctuation/hex string for binary ("01"), runes, or custom glyph sets.
  • Luminous Glitch Hue: Scrambled characters glow in neon phosphor green (GlitchColor) before stabilizing.
  • Interactive Focus Trigger: Re-triggers the decryption sequence whenever the designer clicks or modifies the target field.
  • Whitespace Preservation: Automatically preserves spaces and whitespace formatting so word structures remain legible during the decrypt process.

2. Quick Start

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class SecurityTerminal : MonoBehaviour
{
    [DecryptText(resolveSpeed: 25f, glitchColor: "#00FF66")]
    public string clearanceOverrideCode = "DELTA-OMEGA-99";
}

3. Constructors & Parameters

public DecryptTextAttribute(float resolveSpeed, string glitchColor = "#00FF66")
ParameterTypeDefaultDescription
resolveSpeedfloat20fNumber of characters decoded per second.
glitchColorstring"#00FF66"Hex color string applied to unresolved scrambling characters.

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefaultDescription
ResolveSpeedfloat20fRate of character decoding in glyphs per second.
ScrambleCharsstring`"!@#$%^&*()_+-=[]{};:,.<>?/0123456789ABCDEF"`
GlitchColorstring"#00FF66"Color tint applied to unresolved matrix characters.

5. Production-Grade Example

csharp

    public class DecryptTextExample : ModularMonoBehaviour
    {

        [BoxGroup("1. Standalone Decryption Terminals")]
        [DecryptText(resolveSpeed: 20f, glitchColor: "#00FF66", TriggerOnFocus = true)]
        public string classifiedAgentCodename = "PROJECT-ICARUS-ZERO: AGENT NIGHTSHADE";

        [DecryptText(resolveSpeed: 30f, glitchColor: "#00F0FF", TriggerOnFocus = true)]
        public string alienSignalDecoded = "COSMIC-BEACON: GALAXY-NGC-4826-DISCOVERED";

        [BoxGroup("2. Inside Class Demonstration")]
        public ClassifiedIntelligenceDocket primaryDocket = new ClassifiedIntelligenceDocket();

        [BoxGroup("3. Intelligence Agency Wiretap Manifest")]
        [InfoBox("Demonstrates that [DecryptText] decodes cleanly on fields declared inside wrapper classes inside lists (List<MyClass>).", InspectorMessageType.Info)]
        public List<ClassifiedIntelligenceDocket> wiretapLogs = new List<ClassifiedIntelligenceDocket>
        {
            new ClassifiedIntelligenceDocket { clearanceTier = "CONFIDENTIAL", missionBriefing = "Intercepted diplomatic courier frequency encrypted." },

        };

        {

            public string missionBriefing = "Infiltrate orbital satellite uplink at 0200 hours.";
        }

        [BoxGroup("4. Cryptographic Terminal Controls")]
        [Button("Scramble & Decrypt New Message", BackgroundColor = "#00FF66", TextColor = "#022C22")]
        public void DecryptNewMessage()
        {
            classifiedAgentCodename = "PROJECT-ICARUS-ZERO: AGENT VIPER-STRIKE";
            Debug.Log("<color=#00FF66>[DecryptText]</color> Scramble buffer activated! Matrix characters resolving.");
        }
    }

6. Stacking & Compatibility Matrix

Feature / DomainCompatibilityNotes

7. Performance & Architecture

  • Harmonic Wave Synthesis: Uses closed-form trigonometric combinations (Sin(t) + Cos(1.6t) * 0.5f) rather than costly runtime FFT analysis.
  • Zero Allocations: Evaluates all displacements inline without memory allocations per tick.
Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/FlameText

FlameText

[FlameText] · Target: Field | Property · Category: Typography · Assembly: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes

1. Overview & Value Proposition

In fantasy RPGs, blacksmith forge systems, volcanic survival games, or fiery combat spells, static text feels cold and clinical. Standard Unity inspectors cannot express incinerator heat, molten temperatures, or elemental danger.

[FlameText] animates text characters with erratic vertical Perlin turbulence and a shifting fire color spectrum. Characters flicker upward like tongues of flame, shifting seamlessly from deep crimson ember bases through vivid orange fire bodies to luminous yellow sparks.

2. Quick-Start Snippet

csharp

GlacialFreezeText

[GlacialFreezeText] · Target: Field | Property · Category: Typography · Assembly: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes

1. Overview & Value Proposition

In arctic survival games, cryo weapon systems, cryogenic chambers, or sub-zero blizzard environments, static typography fails to communicate freezing danger. Developers frequently resort to static blue color tints that lack physical tactile urgency.

[GlacialFreezeText] applies an icy crystalline color sheen to field labels, causing characters to shudder with authentic high-frequency micro-tremors (cryo shiver) like teeth chattering in extreme sub-zero cold. It supports both continuous cryo-containment cycles and interactive hover triggers.

Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/[GlitchChopText]

[GlitchChopText]

Applies a horizontal glitch jitter slice displacement to field labels whenever values are modified.

Package: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes Targets: Field | Property AllowMultiple: False Inherited: True Performance: High (Hardware-Accelerated UI Toolkit Transforms)

1. Overview & Value Proposition

What it does: [GlitchChopText] delivers high-impact cybernetic feedback by momentarily chopping and jittering field labels horizontally when values change. Hooked directly into property modification telemetry (TrackPropertyValue), any modification to the field value or collection element row triggers an instantaneous glitch burst. Over Duration (defaulting to 250ms), the primary label shakes horizontally within [-ChopIntensity, +ChopIntensity] pixels (defaulting to ±4px) before snapping cleanly back to rest at (0, 0).

The Problem: When tweaking critical combat thresholds, overclocking multipliers, or volatility variables, standard inspectors provide silent updates without giving tactile or visceral cues that a disruptive value has been committed.

The Solution: Decorating volatile parameters or collection items with [GlitchChopText]:

  • Immediate Value-Change Response: Triggers exclusively upon actual data edits, providing instant visual feedback.
  • Collection Element Cascading: Implements ICollectionElementAttribute so modifying a specific element row in string[] or List<T> glitches only that targeted row.
  • Micro-Jitter Displacement: Randomly shifts horizontal translation via hardware UI Toolkit transforms without causing layout reflow.
  • Configurable Intensity: Fine-tune jitter from subtle 1.5px screen tremors up to violent 8px digital tear displacement.
  • Snappy Auto-Reset: Automatically resets translation to exact origin coordinates upon completion of the burst duration.

2. Quick Start

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class ReactorCore : MonoBehaviour
{
    [GlitchChopText(chopIntensity: 5.0f, Duration = 200f)]
    public float coreTemperature = 3500f;
}

3. Constructors & Parameters

csharp
public GlitchChopTextAttribute(float chopIntensity = 4.0f)
ParameterTypeDefaultDescription
chopIntensityfloat4.0fMaximum horizontal pixel displacement applied during the glitch jitter.

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefaultDescription
ChopIntensityfloat4.0fMaximum horizontal pixel offset in either direction (-ChopIntensity to +ChopIntensity).
Durationfloat250fDuration of the glitch shake burst in milliseconds.
Orderint0Execution and rendering priority order relative to other stacked modular attributes.

5. Production-Grade Example

csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.Runtime;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

namespace MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes.Sample
{
    public class GlitchChopTextExample : ModularMonoBehaviour
    {
        [Title("GlitchChopText Attribute Showcase", "Horizontal text slicing and chromatic displacement glitch")]
        [InfoBox("GlitchChopText imparts authentic cyberpunk video distortion onto text strings whenever edited.", InspectorMessageType.Info)]

        [BoxGroup("1. Standalone Glitch Slicing Fields")]
        [GlitchChopText(chopIntensity: 4.0f, Duration = 250f)]
        public string memorySectorLog = "CORRUPT_SECTOR_0x7F: NEURAL_OVERFLOW_DETECTED";

        [GlitchChopText(chopIntensity: 6.0f, Duration = 350f)]
        public string radiationWarningPrompt = "CRITICAL: REACTOR CORE SHUTDOWN IMMINENT";

        [BoxGroup("2. Inside Class Demonstration")]
        public CorruptedTelemetryRecord primaryError = new CorruptedTelemetryRecord();

        [BoxGroup("3. Cyberware Malfunction Audit Manifest")]
        [InfoBox("Demonstrates that [GlitchChopText] operates cleanly on fields declared inside wrapper classes inside lists (List<MyClass>).", InspectorMessageType.Info)]
        public List<CorruptedTelemetryRecord> diagnosticHistory = new List<CorruptedTelemetryRecord>
        {
            new CorruptedTelemetryRecord { faultCode = "ERR_MEMORY_LEAK_01", errorStackTrace = "Heap overflow in optical targeting buffer." },
            new CorruptedTelemetryRecord { faultCode = "ERR_VOLTAGE_SPIKE_02", errorStackTrace = "Thermal throttle engaged on neural coprocessor." }
        };

        [Serializable]
        public class CorruptedTelemetryRecord
        {
            public string faultCode = "ERR_STACK_DISPLACEMENT_99";

            [GlitchChopText(chopIntensity: 5.0f, Duration = 300f)]
            public string errorStackTrace = "SYNAPSE_FAULT at address 0xFEEDC0DE in core 3";
        }

        [BoxGroup("4. Cybernetic Fault Injection Controls")]
        [Button("Inject Digital Glitch Noise", BackgroundColor = "#C026D3", TextColor = "#FDF4FF")]
        public void InjectGlitch()
        {
            memorySectorLog = $"CORRUPT_SECTOR_0x{UnityEngine.Random.Range(0x1000, 0xFFFF):X}: NEURAL_DESYNC";
            Debug.LogWarning("<color=#F472B6>[GlitchChopText]</color> Digital glitch noise injected!");
        }
    }
}

Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/[GradientTextFlow]

[GradientTextFlow]

Animates field label typography with a smooth, continuously shifting multi-stop iridescent color gradient flow.

Package: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes Targets: Field | Property AllowMultiple: False Inherited: True Performance: High (Hardware-Accelerated UI Toolkit StyleColor Transitions)

1. Overview & Value Proposition

What it does: [GradientTextFlow] transforms static field labels into dynamic iridescent typographic headers. It cycles the label's text color continuously through a 3-stop harmonic gradient spectrum (Color1 → Color2 → Color3 → Color2 → Color1) driven by sinusoidal time modulation (t = (sin(time · Speed) + 1) / (2)). Operating at a customizable cycle frequency (Speed, defaulting to 1.2x), it brings vibrant, fluid visual energy to legendary attributes, elemental damage headers, or collection element rows.

The Problem: Highlighting elite game tiers—such as Mythic rarity, Celestial blessings, or Prismatic elements—typically requires bulky banners or custom info boxes that take up valuable vertical screen real estate in already crowded inspectors.

The Solution: Decorating elite fields or collection items with [GradientTextFlow]:

  • Fluid Color Transition: Interpolates across three distinct color stops with zero perceptible color banding.
  • Collection Element Cascading: Implements ICollectionElementAttribute so every string or socket slot in collections cycles through shimmering chromatic gradients.
  • Ultra-Lightweight: Directly mutates label.style.color without reconstructing text elements or causing layout reflows.
  • Customizable Palettes: Supports fully custom hex codes to match game aesthetics (e.g. Synthwave pink/cyan/yellow, Holy gold/white/blue, or Toxic green/lime/cyan).
  • Adjustable Wave Cadence: Fine-tune oscillation velocity from slow ethereal drifting to fast vibrant pulses.

2. Quick Start

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class MythicItemDefinition : MonoBehaviour
{
    [GradientTextFlow("#FF007F", "#00D2FF", "#FFE600", speed: 1.5f)]
    public string mythicRelicName = "Crown of the Sun King";
}

3. Constructors & Parameters

csharp
public GradientTextFlowAttribute()

public GradientTextFlowAttribute(string color1, string color2, string color3 = "#FFE600", float speed = 1.2f)
ParameterTypeDefaultDescription
color1string"#FF007F"Starting primary color stop of the gradient cycle.
color2string"#00D2FF"Secondary middle color stop of the gradient wave.
color3string"#FFE600"Third color stop of the shifting gradient flow.
speedfloat1.2fModulation frequency speed multiplier.

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefaultDescription
Color1string"#FF007F"Hex color string for the first chromatic stop.
Color2string"#00D2FF"Hex color string for the secondary middle stop.
Color3string"#FFE600"Hex color string for the final third stop.
Speedfloat1.2fSpeed of gradient shifting cycles per second.
Orderint0Execution and rendering priority order relative to other stacked modular attributes.

Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/GravityDropText

GravityDropText

[GravityDropText] · Target: Field | Property · Category: Typography · Assembly: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes

1. Overview & Value Proposition

In arcade cabinets, pinball machines, platformer games, or physics-driven puzzlers, standard text displays lack physical weight. When a player achieves a jackpot or unlocks a rare item, static text fails to deliver the celebratory impact of a physical drop.

[GravityDropText] simulates authentic Newtonian gravity physics. When triggered, characters drop down from above the baseline with gravitational acceleration (9.8 m/s²), bouncing and settling to rest with realistic coefficient-of-restitution damping and impact color flashes.

2. Quick-Start Snippet

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class PinballScoreboard : MonoBehaviour
{
    [GravityDropText(14f, 9.8f, 0.45f)]
    public int JackpotScore = 1500000;
}

3. Constructors & Signatures

csharp
// Default constructor (14px drop height, 9.8 gravity, 0.45 bounciness)
public GravityDropTextAttribute();

// Parameterized constructor with custom drop height, gravity acceleration, and bounciness
public GravityDropTextAttribute(float dropHeight, float gravity = 9.8f, float bounciness = 0.45f);

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefault ValueDescription
DropHeightfloat14fInitial vertical drop displacement in pixels above the baseline.
Gravityfloat9.8fDownward gravitational acceleration factor.
Bouncinessfloat0.45fCoefficient of restitution (0.0 to 1.0) governing bounce height decay.
StaggerDelayfloat35fStagger delay in milliseconds between consecutive falling characters.
ImpactColorstring"#F59E0B"Flash highlight color applied upon ground contact (hex code).

5. Production-Grade Example

csharp

    public int JackpotPoints = 1250000;

    [BoxGroup("Arcade Scores")]
    [GravityDropText(20f, 14f, 0.6f, StaggerDelay = 45f, ImpactColor = "#EC4899")]
    public string BossLootBanner = "LEGENDARY_RELIC_ACQUIRED";

    [BoxGroup("Leaderboard")]
    public List<ScoreSlot> HighScorers = new List<ScoreSlot>
    {
        new ScoreSlot("ACE_GUNNER_X", "GRANDMASTER"),
        new ScoreSlot("SHADOW_VIPER_99", "DIAMOND")
    };

    [Serializable]
    public class ScoreSlot
    {
        [GravityDropText(14f, 9.8f, 0.45f)]
        public string PilotCallsign;

        public string RankTier;

        public ScoreSlot(string callsign, string tier)
        {
            PilotCallsign = callsign;
            RankTier = tier;
        }
    }
}

7. Performance & Architecture

  • Sleep on Rest: Animation loop halts automatically once all letters reach velocity threshold < 1.5 px/s.

  • Sub-character Boundary Antialiasing: Interpolates colors smoothly between adjacent characters at the sweep boundary (blend = wipeHead - index).
  • Zero Transform Impact: Only mutates style.color, ensuring 0 ms layout reflow cost.
Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/[NeonSignFlicker]

[NeonSignFlicker]

Recreates vintage neon sign typography with vivid gas-tube luminescence and intermittent sputtering flickers.

Package: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes Targets: Field | Property AllowMultiple: False Inherited: True Performance: High (Hardware-Accelerated UI Toolkit StyleColor Transitions)

1. Overview & Value Proposition

What it does: [NeonSignFlicker] replicates the imperfect, retro aesthetic of electrical neon gas tubes in the Unity Inspector. It applies bold typographic weight to the primary field label and washes it in a radiant neon hue (NeonColor, defaulting to hot magenta #FF007F). Periodically, evaluated on a 32ms cadence, erratic voltage dips are simulated: if a random check falls below FlickerRate (defaulting to 0.25), the label momentarily sputters to a dim, erratic transparency (alpha between 0.2 and 0.6) before flashing back to full brightness.

The Problem: Cyberpunk, 80s retro, horror, or noir projects frequently lack atmospheric flavor in editor tools. Static magenta or cyan text labels feel like plain flat UI elements rather than glowing electrical signs buzzing in a dark alley.

The Solution: Decorating themed parameters or collection items with [NeonSignFlicker]:

  • Authentic Voltage Sputter: Delivers organic, non-uniform flickers modeled after dying gas-discharge lamps.
  • Collection Element Cascading: Implements ICollectionElementAttribute so every street sign or signboard slot in string collections flickers with independent voltage instability.
  • Bold Tube Typography: Automatically applies FontStyle.Bold to emulate thick glass neon tube filaments.
  • Adjustable Voltage Instability: Tune FlickerRate from a rock-steady hum (0.05) to a dying, erratic strobe (0.50).
  • Vibrant Spectrum: Supports any neon hex color—from hot pink and radioactive green to amber and cobalt blue.

2. Quick Start

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class CyberpunkDiner : MonoBehaviour
{
    [NeonSignFlicker(neonColor: "#FF007F", FlickerRate = 0.2f)]
    public string dinerSignboardName = "NEON DREAMS DINER";
}

3. Constructors & Parameters

csharp
public NeonSignFlickerAttribute(string neonColor = "#FF007F")
ParameterTypeDefaultDescription
neonColorstring"#FF007F"Hex color string for the illuminated neon gas tube text.

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefaultDescription
NeonColorstring"#FF007F"Hex color code applied to the bold neon typography.
FlickerRatefloat0.25fProbability (0.0 to 1.0) of a sputtering voltage drop occurring on each tick.
Orderint0Execution and rendering priority order relative to other stacked modular attributes.

Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/[SlotMachineReelText]

[SlotMachineReelText]

Simulates a mechanical slot machine cylinder reel locking sequence with animated spinning status badges upon value modification.

Package: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes Targets: Field | Property AllowMultiple: False Inherited: True Performance: High (Hardware-Accelerated UI Toolkit Transitions)

1. Overview & Value Proposition

What it does: [SlotMachineReelText] applies a tactile casino slot reel dynamic to enum, numeric, or string properties and collection element rows. Anchored to the top-right edge of the serialized field, it renders an animated mechanical reel status tag. At rest, it displays a dimmed 🎰 LOCKED badge. Whenever the field value is changed—whether through a dropdown pick, slider drag, or procedural script modification—the badge springs into active motion, switching to 🎰 SPINNING... in a warm amber glow (HighlightColor, defaulting to #F59E0B) for SpinDuration (defaulting to 450ms) before clicking firmly back into the locked state.

The Problem: In RNG drop tables, loot box generators, procedural roll configurations, or random perk pickers, selecting a random value or enum feels mundane. Standard inspector fields lack the ceremonial suspense and physical weight of a mechanical slot reel click.

The Solution: Decorating procedural or randomized values with [SlotMachineReelText]:

  • Mechanical Reel State Feedback: Visually differentiates active spinning state (🎰 SPINNING...) from locked commitments (🎰 LOCKED).
  • Collection Element Cascading: Implements ICollectionElementAttribute so each element row in Enum[], string[], or List<T> spins independently on value change.
  • Real-Time Property Tracking: Automatically detects edits via TrackPropertyValue without requiring explicit method calls.
  • Vibrant Accent Highlight: Illuminates in luminous amber (HighlightColor) during the active spin interval.
  • Non-Destructive Layout: Maintains standard field alignment with an unobtrusive right-aligned status indicator.

2. Quick Start

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class CasinoLootBox : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SlotMachineReelText(spinDuration: 500f, HighlightColor = "#F59E0B")]
    public string rolledPrize = "Jackpot Diamond Ring";
}

3. Constructors & Parameters

csharp
public SlotMachineReelTextAttribute(float spinDuration = 450f)
public SlotMachineReelTextAttribute(string[] options, float spinDuration = 450f)
public SlotMachineReelTextAttribute(params string[] options)
ParameterTypeDefaultDescription
spinDurationfloat450fDuration in milliseconds that the reel spins before locking into place.
optionsstring[]nullCandidate symbols or reel items to cycle through during the spin sequence.

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefaultDescription
SpinDurationfloat450fTotal spin sequence duration in milliseconds.
HighlightColorstring"#F59E0B"Hex color string for the active spinning status badge and text highlight.
Optionsstring[]nullCustom string array of symbols or states cycled across during the reel spin animation.
Orderint0Execution and rendering priority order relative to other stacked modular attributes.

5. Production-Grade Example

csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.Runtime;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

namespace MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes.Sample
{
    public class SlotMachineReelTextExample : ModularMonoBehaviour
    {
        public enum GameDifficultyTier { Casual, Normal, Hardcore, Nightmare, Inferno }

        [System.Serializable]
        public class CasinoReelSlot
        {
            public string reelColumnId = "REEL_01";

            [SlotMachineReelText(spinDuration: 550f, HighlightColor = "#F59E0B")]
            public string jackpotSymbol = "CHERRY_TRIPLE";

            [Range(1, 100)]
            public int payoutMultiplier = 25;
        }

        [Title("SlotMachineReelText Attribute Showcase", "Mechanical cylinder drum spinning text")]
        [InfoBox("SlotMachineReelText imparts physical casino mechanical drum motion to fields.", InspectorMessageType.Info)]

        [BoxGroup("1. Standalone Casino Drum Selectors")]
        [SlotMachineReelText(spinDuration: 500f, HighlightColor = "#F59E0B")]
        public GameDifficultyTier campaignDifficulty = GameDifficultyTier.Normal;

        [SlotMachineReelText(spinDuration: 400f, HighlightColor = "#10B981")]
        public string selectedHeroClass = "Sorcerer";

        [SlotMachineReelText(spinDuration: 650f, HighlightColor = "#FBBF24")]
        public string grandPrizeTier = "777 ROYAL DIAMOND JACKPOT";

        [BoxGroup("2. Inside Class Demonstration")]
        public CasinoReelSlot highRollerSlot = new CasinoReelSlot();

        [BoxGroup("3. Active 3-Reel Slot Bank")]
        [InfoBox("SlotMachineReelText cascades to collection elements, spinning mechanical drum options across individual list rows.", InspectorMessageType.Info)]
        [SlotMachineReelText(spinDuration: 500f, HighlightColor = "#F59E0B")]
        public GameDifficultyTier[] difficultyRoster = new GameDifficultyTier[]
        {
            GameDifficultyTier.Normal,
            GameDifficultyTier.Hardcore,
            GameDifficultyTier.Nightmare
        };

        public List<CasinoReelSlot> activeLootReels = new List<CasinoReelSlot>
        {
            new CasinoReelSlot { reelColumnId = "LEFT_REEL", jackpotSymbol = "BAR_DOUBLE", payoutMultiplier = 10 },
            new CasinoReelSlot { reelColumnId = "CENTER_REEL", jackpotSymbol = "BELL_GOLDEN", payoutMultiplier = 20 },
            new CasinoReelSlot { reelColumnId = "RIGHT_REEL", jackpotSymbol = "CROWN_ROYAL", payoutMultiplier = 50 }
        };

        [BoxGroup("4. High Roller Casino Playground")]
        [Button("Spin All Mechanical Reels", BackgroundColor = "#D97706", TextColor = "#FFFBEB")]
        public void SpinAllReels()
        {
            campaignDifficulty = (GameDifficultyTier)Random.Range(0, 5);
            Debug.Log("<color=#FBBF24>[SlotMachineReelText]</color> 🎰 All mechanical reels spinning across the payout payline!");
        }
    }
}

7. Collection Cascading & Custom Serializer Discipline

Context / ArchitectureSupportBehavior & Guidelines
Collection Element CascadingSupported (ICollectionElementAttribute)Cascades down to individual collection rows (Enum[], string[], List<T>), rendering individual spinning drum badges beside each element row.
Target Element RolesSupportedCollectionElementRole.Element, Value, NullableValue, TupleItem.
Direct CollectionsSupportedOperates on direct enum arrays (TEnum[]), string arrays, and numeric lists.
Nested Serializable ClassesSupportedFully spins and locks inside child classes and structs declared within lists.
Custom SerializersSupportedSeamlessly functions in AdvancedList<T>, SerializableDictionary<K, V>, and SerializableTuple<...>.
Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/SonarPingText

SonarPingText

[SonarPingText] · Target: Field | Property · Category: Typography · Assembly: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes

1. Overview & Value Proposition

In subsea naval games, deep-ocean exploration, sonar sensor arrays, or submarine simulators, static text feels detached from underwater hydroacoustics. Developers need intuitive visual cues that indicate live listening arrays, acoustic contacts, or active echo-ranging pings.

[SonarPingText] sweeps a bright acoustic sonar or radar pulse across field label characters. Energized letters light up with a luminous crest highlight and leave an authentic decaying phosphor trail, mirroring the sweep of a naval hydrophone or planar position indicator (PPI).

2. Quick-Start Snippet

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class SubmarineAvionics : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SonarPingText(2.5f, 22f, "#38BDF8")]
    public float SonarDepthMeters = 8450.0f;
}

3. Constructors & Signatures

csharp
// Default constructor (2.5s ping interval, 22 char/s speed, sonar cyan)
public SonarPingTextAttribute();

// Parameterized constructor with custom ping interval, propagation speed, and ping color
public SonarPingTextAttribute(float pingInterval, float pingSpeed = 22f, string pingColor = "#38BDF8");

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefault ValueDescription
PingIntervalfloat2.5fInterval between acoustic sonar pings in seconds.
PingSpeedfloat22fCharacter propagation speed of the ping wave.
PingColorstring"#38BDF8"Acoustic ping beam highlight color at the wave crest (hex code).
TrailColorstring"#0284C7"Fading phosphor decay trail color (hex code).
ContinuousbooltrueWhen true, pings continuously on the defined interval; otherwise triggers only on hover.

5. Production-Grade Example

csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.Runtime;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class NavalSonarStation : ModularMonoBehaviour
{
    [Title("Hydroacoustic Station", "Acoustic ping sweeps and ocean trench telemetry", BackgroundColor = "#0c2d48", TextColor = "#38BDF8")]

    [BoxGroup("Acoustic Telemetry")]
    [SonarPingText(2.5f, 22f, "#38BDF8")]
    public float TrenchDepth = 10994.0f;

    [BoxGroup("Acoustic Telemetry")]
    [SonarPingText(1.8f, 28f, "#00E5FF", TrailColor = "#00527A")]
    public string ActiveSonarContact = "ACOUSTIC_BOGEY_04";

    [BoxGroup("Ocean Transponders")]
    public List<TransponderBeacon> Beacons = new List<TransponderBeacon>
    {
        new TransponderBeacon("BUOY_PACIFIC_TRENCH", "PINGING"),
        new TransponderBeacon("BUOY_ATLANTIC_RIDGE", "ECHO_LOCKED")
    };

    [Serializable]
    public class TransponderBeacon
    {
        [SonarPingText(2.0f, 22f, "#38BDF8")]
        public string BeaconName;

        public string EchoState;

        public TransponderBeacon(string name, string state)
        {
            BeaconName = name;
            EchoState = state;
        }
    }
}

6. Stacking & Compatibility Matrix

Feature / DomainCompatibilityNotes
[BoxGroup] & [AdvancedClass]CompatibleRetains clean container framing during active phosphor ping sweeps.
[ReadOnly]CompatibleOperates normally on locked sensor readouts and depth coordinates.
Lists (List<TClass>)CompatibleSafe segmented container prevents DOM corruption in virtualized rows.

7. Performance & Architecture

  • Linear Wavefront Mathematics: Computes distance from wave crest per tick without heap allocations.
  • Color Decay Falloff: Smoothly interpolates from ping crest to phosphor trail to base inspector color.
Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/TrackingStretchText

TrackingStretchText

[TrackingStretchText] · Target: Field | Property · Category: Typography · Assembly: MSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes

1. Overview & Value Proposition

Standard inspector labels feature fixed character kerning that remains static regardless of user interaction. In luxury, editorial, or prestige game interfaces (e.g. RPG item cards, boss title cards, achievements), subtle kinetic typography elevates developer ergonomics.

[TrackingStretchText] smoothly expands and contracts the horizontal spacing between letters with an elastic spring animation whenever the user hovers over the field. It also supports continuous breathing modes for hero parameters and title headings.

2. Quick-Start Snippet

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class RelicInspector : MonoBehaviour
{
    [TrackingStretchText(5f, 12f, "#38BDF8")]
    public string RelicTitle = "CHRONICLES OF AVALON";
}

3. Constructors & Signatures

csharp
// Default constructor (4px stretch tracking, spring speed 12, sky blue highlight)
public TrackingStretchTextAttribute();

// Parameterized constructor with custom stretch spacing, spring speed, and highlight color
public TrackingStretchTextAttribute(float stretchTracking, float springSpeed = 12f, string highlightColor = "#38BDF8");

4. Properties & Customization Table

PropertyTypeDefault ValueDescription
StretchTrackingfloat4fMaximum horizontal spacing in pixels added between characters when expanded.
SpringSpeedfloat12fSpring lerp interpolation speed for expansion and contraction.
HighlightColorstring"#38BDF8"Accent color applied to text in the expanded hover state (hex code).
ContinuousboolfalseWhen true, continuously pulses tracking width in an idle breathing wave.

5. Production-Grade Example

csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.Runtime;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class HeroPrestigeInspector : ModularMonoBehaviour
{
    [Title("Editorial Showcase", "Kinetic kerning on inspector fields", BackgroundColor = "#082f49", TextColor = "#38BDF8")]

    [BoxGroup("Hero Attributes")]
    [TrackingStretchText(5f, 12f, "#38BDF8")]
    public string ChampionName = "VALERIUS THE UNVANQUISHED";

    [BoxGroup("Hero Attributes")]
    [TrackingStretchText(4f, 10f, "#FBBF24", Continuous = true)]
    public float PrestigeMultiplier = 3.5f;

    [BoxGroup("Equipped Titles")]
    public List<TitleCard> ActiveTitles = new List<TitleCard>
    {
        new TitleCard("DEFENDER OF SUNSPIRE", 1200),
        new TitleCard("VOID WALKER MASTER", 3500)
    };

    [Serializable]
    public class TitleCard
    {
        [TrackingStretchText(4.5f, 14f, "#F472B6")]
        public string TitleDesignation;

        public int HonorScore;

        public TitleCard(string designation, int score)
        {
            TitleDesignation = designation;
            HonorScore = score;
        }
    }
}

6. Stacking & Compatibility Matrix

Feature / DomainCompatibilityNotes
[BoxGroup] & [AdvancedClass]CompatibleDynamic margin expands without overflowing parent card boundaries.
[ReadOnly]CompatibleText continues to stretch smoothly on hover even when editing is locked.
Lists (List<TClass>)CompatibleSafely restores original margin during virtualized row recycling.

7. Performance & Architecture

  • Sub-pixel Layout Safety: Only adjusts marginRight on child letter elements, preventing layout recalculation on sibling controls.
  • Damped Spring Math: Fast analytical lerp runs at 60 fps only during active transitions, sleeping when rest position is reached.
Animated Attributes/Kinetic Typography/[WavyText]

[WavyText]

Animates characters in field labels with a fluid, undulating vertical sine wave oscillation.

1. Overview & Value Proposition

What it does: [WavyText] converts static field labels into an undulating water wave ripple. It splits the label text into individual character elements and continuously oscillates them along the Y-axis using sinusoidal phase offsets (y = sin(time · 2π · WaveFrequency + i · CharacterPhaseShift) · WaveAmplitude). With customizable vertical amplitude (WaveAmplitude, defaulting to 3.5px), frequency (WaveFrequency, defaulting to 2.5 Hz), and character phase spacing (CharacterPhaseShift, defaulting to 0.4 rad), it gives aquatic, psychedelic, or whimsical properties a floating motion. It also supports hover-only triggering (Continuous = false).

The Problem: Aquatic systems, wind simulations, audio waveforms, or dreamlike fantasy mechanics often feel dry when presented through rigid, square inspector text. Static labels provide no visual hint that an underlying system is undulating or fluid.

The Solution: Decorating liquid or oscillatory fields with [WavyText]:

  • Fluid Wave Motion: Creates a rolling vertical sine wave across the text without layout jitter or text clipping.
  • Continuous or Hover-Only: Set Continuous = true for ambient kinetic presence, or false for interactive hover-triggered ripples.
  • Adjustable Wave Dynamics: Tune peak amplitude from gentle 1.5px water laps up to pronounced 6px tidal crests.
  • Independent Phase Offsets: Staggers individual character delays via CharacterPhaseShift to maintain seamless wave continuity across strings of any length.

2. Quick Start

csharp
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class OceanBuoyancyManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    [WavyText(amplitude: 4.0f, frequency: 2.2f)]
    public float oceanWaveHeight = 2.75f;
}

3. Compatibility & Specification

SpecificationValue
PackageMSUltimate-AnimatedAttributes Addon
Attribute Target`Field
Supported TypesAny serializable field or property
Allow MultipleNo (AllowMultiple = false)
InheritedYes
Collection SupportSupported on fields in wrapper classes (List<MyClass>); Direct collection cascading is intentionally not supported.
UI Toolkit CompatibleYes

4. Constructors & Parameters

csharp
public WavyTextAttribute()

public WavyTextAttribute(float amplitude, float frequency)
ParameterTypeDefaultDescription
amplitudefloat3.5fPeak vertical amplitude displacement in pixels.
frequencyfloat2.5fFrequency of the sine wave in cycles per second.

5. Properties & Customization Options

PropertyTypeDefaultDescription
WaveAmplitudefloat3.5fMaximum vertical pixel displacement from baseline.
WaveFrequencyfloat2.5fOscillation frequency in complete wave cycles per second.
CharacterPhaseShiftfloat0.4fPhase offset between consecutive characters in radians.
ContinuousbooltrueIf true, ripples indefinitely; if false, ripples only while hovered by the cursor.
Orderint0Execution and rendering priority order relative to other stacked modular attributes.

6. Production-Grade Example

csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using MaharajaStudio.MSUltimate.AnimatedAttributes;

public class LiquidBiomeController : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("Aquatic Simulation Parameters")]
    [WavyText(amplitude: 3.0f, frequency: 1.8f, CharacterPhaseShift = 0.35f)]
    public float fluidViscosity = 1.2f;

    [WavyText(amplitude: 4.5f, frequency: 3.0f, Continuous = true)]
    public string currentTideState = "HIGH_TIDE";

    [Header("Interactive Dream Properties")]
    [WavyText(amplitude: 5.0f, frequency: 2.5f, Continuous = false)]
    public float hallucinationStrength = 0.85f;

    [Header("Wrapper Classes in Lists")]
    public List<MarineSpecimen> marineSpecimens = new List<MarineSpecimen>
    {
        new MarineSpecimen { specimenName = "Bioluminescent Jellyfish", depthMeters = 1200f }
    };

    [Serializable]
    public class MarineSpecimen
    {
        [WavyText(amplitude: 3.5f, frequency: 2.2f)]
        public string specimenName = "Pelagic Leviathan";

        public float depthMeters = 3000f;
    }
}
